    WHEN 1 THEN N'EnAttente'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'Confirme'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Echoue'

END AS [Statut], CASE [p].[ModePaiement]
    WHEN 1 THEN N'OrangeMoney'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'MtnMoney'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Especes'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'M2u'
    WHEN 5 THEN N'SaraMoney'
    ELSE CAST([p].[ModePaiement] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [ModePaiement], [b0].[Reference] AS [BienReference]
FROM [Paiements] AS [p]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[BienId], [c].[LocataireId]
    FROM [Contrats] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [c0] ON [p].[ContratId] = [c0].[Id]
